```python
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton#providerStarButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #999999;
                }
                QPushButton#providerStarButton:hover {
                    color: #666666;
                }
            """)
            self.star_button.setVisible(True)
        else:
            self.star_button.setVisible(False)
            
        if self._expansion_popup and self._expansion_popup.isVisible():
            self._expansion_popup._update_star_display()
    
    def _update_style(self):
        """Update appearance based on selection state."""
        # 1. Base style
        base_style = COMMON_BASE_STYLE + f"""
            QFrame#providerItem {{
                background-color: {Colors.NORMAL_BG};
                border: 1px solid transparent;
                border-radius: 4px;
            }}
        """

        # 2. State specific style
        if self._is_selected:
            status_style = f"""
                QFrame#providerItem {{
                    background-color: {Colors.SELECTED_BG};
                    border: 1px solid {Colors.SELECTED_BORDER};
                }}
                QLabel#providerName {{
                    color: {Colors.SELECTED_TEXT};
                }}
            """
        else:
            status_style = f"""
                QFrame#providerItem:hover {{
                    background-color: {Colors.HOVER_BG};
                    border-color: {Colors.HOVER_BORDER};
                }}
                QLabel#providerName {{
                    color: {Colors.TEXT_PRIMARY};
                }}
            """
        
        # 3. Combine
        self.setStyleSheet(base_style + status_style)
        
        if self._expansion_popup and self._expansion_popup.isVisible():
            self._expansion_popup.update_appearance()
    
    def _on_star_clicked(self):
        """Handle star button click."""
        self._is_starred = not self._is_starred
        self._update_star_display()
        self.star_toggled.emit(self.provider_name, self._is_starred)
    
    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            # Check if click was not on star button
            star_rect = self.star_button.geometry()
            if not star_rect.contains(event.pos()):
                self.clicked.emit(self.provider_name)
        super().mousePressEvent(event)
    
    def enterEvent(self, event):
        self._is_hovered = True
        self._update_star_display()
        self._check_show_expanded()
        super().enterEvent(event)
    
    def leaveEvent(self, event):
        self._is_hovered = False
        self._update_star_display()
        
        # Don't hide if moving to the popup
        if self._expansion_popup and self._expansion_popup.isVisible():
            # Check if mouse is within popup rect
            cursor_pos = QCursor.pos()
            if self._expansion_popup.geometry().contains(cursor_pos):
                return
            self._expansion_popup.hide()
            
        super().leaveEvent(event)
        
    def _check_show_expanded(self):
        """Check if we need to show expanded popup."""
        # Check text width
        fm = self.name_label.fontMetrics()
        text_width = fm.boundingRect(self.provider_name).width()
        available_width = self.name_label.width()
        
        # If text is truncated or just for consistent "float out" effect
        if text_width > available_width:
            if not self._expansion_popup:
                self._expansion_popup = ExpandedProviderItem(self)
            
            # Position over the item
            global_pos = self.mapToGlobal(self.rect().topLeft())
            
            # Calculate required width
            req_width = text_width + 60 # padding + star button
            req_width = max(req_width, self.width())  # Ensure at least as wide as original
            
            self._expansion_popup.resize(req_width, self.height())
            self._expansion_popup.move(global_pos)
            self._expansion_popup.update_appearance()
            self._expansion_popup.show()

    def set_selected(self, selected: bool):
        """Set selection state."""
        if self._is_selected != selected:
            self._is_selected = selected
            self._update_style()
    
    def set_starred(self, starred: bool):
        """Set starred state without emitting signal."""
        if self._is_starred != starred:
            self._is_starred = starred
            self._update_star_display()
    
    def is_selected(self) -> bool:
        return self._is_selected
    
    def is_starred(self) -> bool:
        return self._is_starred


class ProviderList(QFrame):
    """Scrollable list of providers for the selected model."""
    
    provider_clicked = pyqtSignal(str)  # provider_name
    provider_star_toggled = pyqtSignal(str, bool)  # provider_name, new_status
    
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("providerList")
        self._provider_items: dict[str, ProviderItem] = {}
        self._current_selection: str | None = None
        self.init_ui()
    
    def init_ui(self):
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        main_layout.setSpacing(0)
        
        # Header
        header = QLabel("Providers:")
        header.setObjectName("providerListHeader")
        header.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel#providerListHeader {
                color: #666666;
                font-size: 10px;
                padding: 6px 8px;
                background-color: #f3f3f3;
                border-bottom: 1px solid #e0e0e0;
            }
        """)
        main_layout.addWidget(header)
        
        # Scroll area
        self.scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        self.scroll_area.setObjectName("providerScrollArea")
        self.scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.scroll_area.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.scroll_area.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAsNeeded)
        self.scroll_area.setStyleSheet("""
            QScrollArea#providerScrollArea {
                background-color: #f7f7f7;
                border: none;
            }
            QScrollBar:vertical {
                width: 8px;
                background: #f7f7f7;
            }
            QScrollBar::handle:vertical {
                background: #c0c0c0;
                border-radius: 4px;
                min-height: 20px;
            }
            QScrollBar::handle:vertical:hover {
                background: #a0a0a0;
            }
            QScrollBar::add-line:vertical, QScrollBar::sub-line:vertical {
                height: 0;
            }
        """)
        
        # Container for provider items
        self.container = QWidget()
        self.container.setObjectName("providerContainer")
        self.container.setStyleSheet("background-color: #f7f7f7;")
        self.items_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.container)
        self.items_layout.setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4)
        self.items_layout.setSpacing(4)
        self.items_layout.addStretch()
        
        # Placeholder
        self.placeholder = QLabel("Select a model")
        self.placeholder.setObjectName("providerPlaceholder")
        self.placeholder.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.placeholder.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel#providerPlaceholder {
                color: #999999;
                font-size: 10px;
                padding: 20px;
            }
        """)
        self.items_layout.insertWidget(0, self.placeholder)
        
        self.scroll_area.setWidget(self.container)
        main_layout.addWidget(self.scroll_area)
        
        self.setFixedWidth(140)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#providerList {
                background-color: #f7f7f7;
                border-left: 1px solid #e0e0e0;
            }
        """)
    
    def set_providers(self, providers: List[str], starred_provider: Optional[str] = None):
        """Set the list of providers to display."""
        # Clear existing items
        self._provider_items.clear()
        self._current_selection = None
        
        while self.items_layout.count() > 1:  # Keep stretch
            item = self.items_layout.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()
        
        if not providers:
            # Show placeholder
            self.placeholder = QLabel("No providers")
            self.placeholder.setObjectName("providerPlaceholder")
            self.placeholder.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
            self.placeholder.setStyleSheet("""
                QLabel#providerPlaceholder {
                    color: #999999;
                    font-size: 10px;
                    padding: 20px;
                }
            """)
            self.items_layout.insertWidget(0, self.placeholder)
            return
        
        # Add provider items
        for provider_name in providers:
            is_starred = provider_name == starred_provider
            item = ProviderItem(provider_name, is_starred=is_starred)
            item.clicked.connect(self._on_item_clicked)
            item.star_toggled.connect(self._on_star_toggled)
            self.items_layout.insertWidget(self.items_layout.count() - 1, item)
            self._provider_items[provider_name] = item
            
            # Auto-select starred provider
            if is_starred:
                self._current_selection = provider_name
                item.set_selected(True)
        
        # If no starred, select first
        if self._current_selection is None and providers:
            first_provider = providers[0]
            self._current_selection = first_provider
            self._provider_items[first_provider].set_selected(True)
    
    def _on_item_clicked(self, provider_name: str):
        """Handle provider item click."""
        self.select_provider(provider_name)
        self.provider_clicked.emit(provider_name)
    
    def _on_star_toggled(self, provider_name: str, new_status: bool):
        """Handle star toggle - ensure only one is starred."""
        if new_status:
            # Unstar all others
            for name, item in self._provider_items.items():
                if name != provider_name and item.is_starred():
                    item.set_starred(False)
        
        self.provider_star_toggled.emit(provider_name, new_status)
    
    def select_provider(self, provider_name: str):
        """Select a provider."""
        if self._current_selection == provider_name:
            return
        
        # Deselect previous
        if self._current_selection and self._current_selection in self._provider_items:
            self._provider_items[self._current_selection].set_selected(False)
        
        # Select new
        self._current_selection = provider_name
        if provider_name in self._provider_items:
            self._provider_items[provider_name].set_selected(True)
    
    def get_selected_provider(self) -> Optional[str]:
        return self._current_selection
    
    def get_starred_provider(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """Get currently starred provider name."""
        for name, item in self._provider_items.items():
            if item.is_starred():
                return name
        return None

```

I have completed the requested modifications to the `model_selector` GUI component.

### Summary of Changes

1.  **Layout Alignment (Task 1)**:
    *   Modified `ModelSelectorWidget` to shift the popup position to the left by the width of the Family List (approx. 100px).
    *   This ensures the "Model Card" column aligns with the left edge of the trigger button, allowing the "Family List" to overflow to the left as requested.

2.  **Floating Provider Cards (Task 2)**:
    *   Implemented a "float out" mechanism in `ProviderList`.
    *   When hovering over a provider item with a long name (or any item that exceeds available width), a frameless popup (`ExpandedProviderItem`) appears over it.
    *   This expanded card automatically resizes to fit the full text while maintaining the "Star" and "Select" functionality.

3.  **Hierarchy Adjustment (Task 3)**:
    *   Moved the `header_bar` (containing the Task Badge, "Model:" label, and OK button) from the top-level layout into the `model_column` layout.
    *   The header is now visually part of the central model column, fixed at the top, while the list of models scrolls underneath it.

4.  **Task Badge Renaming (Task 4)**:
    *   Updated `_format_task_name` in `ModelSelectorPopup` to map configuration keys to display names:
        *   `text-chat` → **"Chat"**
        *   `audio-transcription` → **"Audio"**
    *   Added a fallback to the original formatting for unknown tasks.

### Files Modified
*   `src/gui/components/model_selector/model_selector_widget.py`: Adjusted popup positioning logic.
*   `src/gui/components/model_selector/model_selector_popup.py`: Reorganized layout hierarchy and updated task name formatting.
*   `src/gui/components/model_selector/provider_list.py`: Added `ExpandedProviderItem` class and updated `ProviderItem` to handle hover expansion.

---

**User**

ExpandedProviderItem的 leave event 不生效，在鼠标离开onHover、选择其他模型、关闭model selector、乃至切换另一软件至前台全屏时，此item仍然不消失，在屏幕顶层悬浮。

---

**Cursor**

我来查看当前代码状态并修复这个问题。

问题是 `Qt.ToolTip` 窗口的 `leaveEvent` 在很多情况下不会被正确触发。我需要使用定时器轮询检测鼠标位置，并且在各种情况下主动关闭 popup。

```python
"""
Provider List Widget - Right column showing available providers for selected model.
"""

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import (
    QFrame, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QScrollArea, QWidget, QHBoxLayout, 
    QPushButton, QSizePolicy, QApplication
)
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, pyqtSignal, QEvent, QTimer
from PyQt5.QtGui import QFont, QCursor
from typing import List, Optional

from .styles import ModelSelectorColors as Colors, COMMON_BASE_STYLE


class ExpandedProviderItem(QWidget):
    """Floating window for displaying full provider name when truncated."""
    
    def __init__(self, original_item, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.original_item = original_item
        self._check_timer = None
        
        self.setWindowFlags(Qt.ToolTip | Qt.FramelessWindowHint)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_TranslucentBackground)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_ShowWithoutActivating)
        
        self.init_ui()
        self.update_appearance()
        
        # Monitor application state changes (window switching)
        QApplication.instance().applicationStateChanged.connect(self._on_app_state_changed)
        
    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        
        self.frame = QFrame()
        self.frame.setObjectName("expandedProviderFrame")
        layout.addWidget(self.frame)
        
        frame_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.frame)
        frame_layout.setContentsMargins(10, 8, 10, 8)
        frame_layout.setSpacing(6)
        
        # Provider name
        self.name_label = QLabel(self.original_item.provider_name)
        font = QFont()
        font.setPointSize(9)
        self.name_label.setFont(font)
        frame_layout.addWidget(self.name_label)
        
        # Star button
        self.star_button = QPushButton()
        self.star_button.setFixedSize(22, 22)
        self.star_button.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.star_button.clicked.connect(self._on_star_clicked)
        frame_layout.addWidget(self.star_button)
        
        self._update_star_display()
        
    def update_appearance(self):
        # Copy style from original item but ensure we have a background and shadow
        is_selected = self.original_item.is_selected()
        
        bg_color = Colors.SELECTED_BG if is_selected else Colors.HOVER_BG
        border_color = Colors.SELECTED_BORDER if is_selected else Colors.HOVER_BORDER
        text_color = Colors.SELECTED_TEXT if is_selected else Colors.TEXT_PRIMARY
        
        self.frame.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#expandedProviderFrame {{
                background-color: {bg_color};
                border: 1px solid {border_color};
                border-radius: 4px;
            }}
            QLabel {{
                color: {text_color};
            }}
        """)
        
        self._update_star_display()
        
    def _update_star_display(self):
        is_starred = self.original_item.is_starred()
        
        if is_starred:
            self.star_button.setText("★")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #f5a623;
                }
                QPushButton:hover {
                    color: #ffc107;
                }
            """)
        else:
            self.star_button.setText("☆")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #999999;
                }
                QPushButton:hover {
                    color: #666666;
                }
            """)

    def _on_star_clicked(self):
        # Forward click to original item's logic
        self.original_item._on_star_clicked()
        self._update_star_display()

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            star_rect = self.star_button.geometry()
            # Map click to frame coordinates
            click_pos = self.frame.mapFrom(self, event.pos())
            
            if not star_rect.contains(click_pos):
                self.original_item.clicked.emit(self.original_item.provider_name)
        super().mousePressEvent(event)
    
    def showEvent(self, event):
        """Start mouse position polling when shown."""
        super().showEvent(event)
        if self._check_timer is None:
            self._check_timer = QTimer(self)
            self._check_timer.timeout.connect(self._check_mouse_position)
        self._check_timer.start(50)  # Check every 50ms
    
    def hideEvent(self, event):
        """Stop polling when hidden."""
        if self._check_timer:
            self._check_timer.stop()
        super().hideEvent(event)
    
    def _check_mouse_position(self):
        """Check if mouse is still within valid area (popup or original item)."""
        if not self.isVisible():
            return
        
        cursor_pos = QCursor.pos()
        
        # Check if mouse is in this popup
        if self.geometry().contains(cursor_pos):
            return
        
        # Check if mouse is in original item (allow moving between them)
        if self.original_item and self.original_item.isVisible():
            item_global_rect = self.original_item.geometry()
            item_global_rect.moveTopLeft(self.original_item.mapToGlobal(self.original_item.rect().topLeft()))
            if item_global_rect.contains(cursor_pos):
                return
        
        # Mouse left both areas, hide
        self.hide()
    
    def _on_app_state_changed(self, state):
        """Hide when application loses focus."""
        if state != Qt.ApplicationActive:
            self.hide()
    
    def close(self):
        """Cleanup on close."""
        if self._check_timer:
            self._check_timer.stop()
            self._check_timer = None
        try:
            QApplication.instance().applicationStateChanged.disconnect(self._on_app_state_changed)
        except (TypeError, RuntimeError):
            pass  # Already disconnected or app closing
        super().close()


class ProviderItem(QFrame):
    """A single provider item in the provider list."""
    
    clicked = pyqtSignal(str)  # provider_name
    star_toggled = pyqtSignal(str, bool)  # provider_name, new_status
    
    def __init__(self, provider_name: str, is_starred: bool = False, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.provider_name = provider_name
        self._is_starred = is_starred
        self._is_selected = False
        self._is_hovered = False
        self._expansion_popup = None
        
        self.setObjectName("providerItem")
        self.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.setMouseTracking(True)
        self.init_ui()
        self._update_style()
    
    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(10, 8, 10, 8)
        layout.setSpacing(6)
        
        # Provider name
        self.name_label = QLabel(self.provider_name)
        self.name_label.setObjectName("providerName")
        font = QFont()
        font.setPointSize(9)
        self.name_label.setFont(font)
        layout.addWidget(self.name_label, 1)
        
        # Star button
        self.star_button = QPushButton()
        self.star_button.setObjectName("providerStarButton")
        self.star_button.setFixedSize(22, 22)
        self.star_button.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.star_button.clicked.connect(self._on_star_clicked)
        layout.addWidget(self.star_button)
        
        self._update_star_display()
        
        self.setMinimumHeight(38)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed)
    
    def _update_star_display(self):
        """Update star button appearance based on state."""
        if self._is_starred:
            self.star_button.setText("★")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton#providerStarButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #f5a623;
                }
                QPushButton#providerStarButton:hover {
                    color: #ffc107;
                }
            """)
            self.star_button.setVisible(True)
        elif self._is_hovered:
            self.star_button.setText("☆")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton#providerStarButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #999999;
                }
                QPushButton#providerStarButton:hover {
                    color: #666666;
                }
            """)
            self.star_button.setVisible(True)
        else:
            self.star_button.setVisible(False)
            
        if self._expansion_popup and self._expansion_popup.isVisible():
            self._expansion_popup._update_star_display()
    
    def _update_style(self):
        """Update appearance based on selection state."""
        # 1. Base style
        base_style = COMMON_BASE_STYLE + f"""
            QFrame#providerItem {{
                background-color: {Colors.NORMAL_BG};
                border: 1px solid transparent;
                border-radius: 4px;
            }}
        """

        # 2. State specific style
        if self._is_selected:
            status_style = f"""
                QFrame#providerItem {{
                    background-color: {Colors.SELECTED_BG};
                    border: 1px solid {Colors.SELECTED_BORDER};
                }}
                QLabel#providerName {{
                    color: {Colors.SELECTED_TEXT};
                }}
            """
        else:
            status_style = f"""
                QFrame#providerItem:hover {{
                    background-color: {Colors.HOVER_BG};
                    border-color: {Colors.HOVER_BORDER};
                }}
                QLabel#providerName {{
                    color: {Colors.TEXT_PRIMARY};
                }}
            """
        
        # 3. Combine
        self.setStyleSheet(base_style + status_style)
        
        if self._expansion_popup and self._expansion_popup.isVisible():
            self._expansion_popup.update_appearance()
    
    def _on_star_clicked(self):
        """Handle star button click."""
        self._is_starred = not self._is_starred
        self._update_star_display()
        self.star_toggled.emit(self.provider_name, self._is_starred)
    
    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            # Check if click was not on star button
            star_rect = self.star_button.geometry()
            if not star_rect.contains(event.pos()):
                self.clicked.emit(self.provider_name)
        super().mousePressEvent(event)
    
    def enterEvent(self, event):
        self._is_hovered = True
        self._update_star_display()
        self._check_show_expanded()
        super().enterEvent(event)
    
    def leaveEvent(self, event):
        self._is_hovered = False
        self._update_star_display()
        
        # Don't hide if moving to the popup
        if self._expansion_popup and self._expansion_popup.isVisible():
            # Check if mouse is within popup rect
            cursor_pos = QCursor.pos()
            if self._expansion_popup.geometry().contains(cursor_pos):
                return
            self._expansion_popup.hide()
            
        super().leaveEvent(event)
        
    def _check_show_expanded(self):
        """Check if we need to show expanded popup."""
        # Check text width
        fm = self.name_label.fontMetrics()
        text_width = fm.boundingRect(self.provider_name).width()
        available_width = self.name_label.width()
        
        # If text is truncated or just for consistent "float out" effect
        if text_width > available_width:
            if not self._expansion_popup:
                self._expansion_popup = ExpandedProviderItem(self)
            
            # Position over the item
            global_pos = self.mapToGlobal(self.rect().topLeft())
            
            # Calculate required width
            req_width = text_width + 60 # padding + star button
            req_width = max(req_width, self.width())  # Ensure at least as wide as original
            
            self._expansion_popup.resize(req_width, self.height())
            self._expansion_popup.move(global_pos)
            self._expansion_popup.update_appearance()
            self._expansion_popup.show()

    def set_selected(self, selected: bool):
        """Set selection state."""
        if self._is_selected != selected:
            self._is_selected = selected
            self._update_style()
    
    def set_starred(self, starred: bool):
        """Set starred state without emitting signal."""
        if self._is_starred != starred:
            self._is_starred = starred
            self._update_star_display()
    
    def is_selected(self) -> bool:
        return self._is_selected
    
    def is_starred(self) -> bool:
        return self._is_starred


class ProviderList(QFrame):

    provider_clicked = pyqtSignal(str)  # provider_name
    provider_star_toggled = pyqtSignal(str, bool)  # provider_name, new_status

        # Placeholder
        self.placeholder = QLabel("Select a model")
        self.placeholder.setObjectName("providerPlaceholder")
        self.placeholder.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.placeholder.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel#providerPlaceholder {
                color: #999999;
                font-size: 10px;
                padding: 20px;
            }
        """)
        self.items_layout.insertWidget(0, self.placeholder)
        
        self.scroll_area.setWidget(self.container)
        main_layout.addWidget(self.scroll_area)
        
        self.setFixedWidth(140)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#providerList {
                background-color: #f7f7f7;
                border-left: 1px solid #e0e0e0;
            }
        """)
    
    def set_providers(self, providers: List[str], starred_provider: Optional[str] = None):
        """Set the list of providers to display."""
        # Clear existing items
        self._provider_items.clear()
        self._current_selection = None
        
        while self.items_layout.count() > 1:  # Keep stretch
            item = self.items_layout.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()
        
        if not providers:
            # Show placeholder
            self.placeholder = QLabel("No providers")
            self.placeholder.setObjectName("providerPlaceholder")
            self.placeholder.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
            self.placeholder.setStyleSheet("""
                QLabel#providerPlaceholder {
                    color: #999999;
                    font-size: 10px;
                    padding: 20px;
                }
            """)
            self.items_layout.insertWidget(0, self.placeholder)
            return
        
        # Add provider items
        for provider_name in providers:
            is_starred = provider_name == starred_provider
            item = ProviderItem(provider_name, is_starred=is_starred)
            item.clicked.connect(self._on_item_clicked)
            item.star_toggled.connect(self._on_star_toggled)
            self.items_layout.insertWidget(self.items_layout.count() - 1, item)
            self._provider_items[provider_name] = item
            
            # Auto-select starred provider
            if is_starred:
                self._current_selection = provider_name
                item.set_selected(True)
        
        # If no starred, select first
        if self._current_selection is None and providers:
            first_provider = providers[0]
            self._current_selection = first_provider
            self._provider_items[first_provider].set_selected(True)
    
    def _on_item_clicked(self, provider_name: str):
        """Handle provider item click."""
        self.select_provider(provider_name)
        self.provider_clicked.emit(provider_name)
    
    def _on_star_toggled(self, provider_name: str, new_status: bool):
        """Handle star toggle - ensure only one is starred."""
        if new_status:
            # Unstar all others
            for name, item in self._provider_items.items():
                if name != provider_name and item.is_starred():
                    item.set_starred(False)
        
        self.provider_star_toggled.emit(provider_name, new_status)
    
    def select_provider(self, provider_name: str):
        """Select a provider."""
        if self._current_selection == provider_name:
            return
        
        # Deselect previous
        if self._current_selection and self._current_selection in self._provider_items:
            self._provider_items[self._current_selection].set_selected(False)
        
        # Select new
        self._current_selection = provider_name
        if provider_name in self._provider_items:
            self._provider_items[provider_name].set_selected(True)
    
    def get_selected_provider(self) -> Optional[str]:
        return self._current_selection
    
    def get_starred_provider(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """Get currently starred provider name."""
        for name, item in self._provider_items.items():
            if item.is_starred():
                return name
        return None
```

```python
"""
Provider List Widget - Right column showing available providers for selected model.
"""

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import (
    QFrame, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QScrollArea, QWidget, QHBoxLayout, 
    QPushButton, QSizePolicy, QApplication
)
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, pyqtSignal, QEvent, QTimer
from PyQt5.QtGui import QFont, QCursor
from typing import List, Optional

from .styles import ModelSelectorColors as Colors, COMMON_BASE_STYLE


class ExpandedProviderItem(QWidget):
    """Floating window for displaying full provider name when truncated."""
    
    def __init__(self, original_item, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.original_item = original_item
        self._check_timer = None
        
        self.setWindowFlags(Qt.ToolTip | Qt.FramelessWindowHint)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_TranslucentBackground)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_ShowWithoutActivating)
        
        self.init_ui()
        self.update_appearance()
        
        # Monitor application state changes (window switching)
        QApplication.instance().applicationStateChanged.connect(self._on_app_state_changed)
        
    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        
        self.frame = QFrame()
        self.frame.setObjectName("expandedProviderFrame")
        layout.addWidget(self.frame)
        
        frame_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.frame)
        frame_layout.setContentsMargins(10, 8, 10, 8)
        frame_layout.setSpacing(6)
        
        # Provider name
        self.name_label = QLabel(self.original_item.provider_name)
        font = QFont()
        font.setPointSize(9)
        self.name_label.setFont(font)
        frame_layout.addWidget(self.name_label)
        
        # Star button
        self.star_button = QPushButton()
        self.star_button.setFixedSize(22, 22)
        self.star_button.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.star_button.clicked.connect(self._on_star_clicked)
        frame_layout.addWidget(self.star_button)
        
        self._update_star_display()
        
    def update_appearance(self):
        # Copy style from original item but ensure we have a background and shadow
        is_selected = self.original_item.is_selected()
        
        bg_color = Colors.SELECTED_BG if is_selected else Colors.HOVER_BG
        border_color = Colors.SELECTED_BORDER if is_selected else Colors.HOVER_BORDER
        text_color = Colors.SELECTED_TEXT if is_selected else Colors.TEXT_PRIMARY
        
        self.frame.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#expandedProviderFrame {{
                background-color: {bg_color};
                border: 1px solid {border_color};
                border-radius: 4px;
            }}
            QLabel {{
                color: {text_color};
            }}
        """)
        
        self._update_star_display()
        
    def _update_star_display(self):
        is_starred = self.original_item.is_starred()
        
        if is_starred:
            self.star_button.setText("★")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #f5a623;
                }
                QPushButton:hover {
                    color: #ffc107;
                }
            """)
        else:
            self.star_button.setText("☆")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #999999;
                }
                QPushButton:hover {
                    color: #666666;
                }
            """)

    def _on_star_clicked(self):
        # Forward click to original item's logic
        self.original_item._on_star_clicked()
        self._update_star_display()

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            star_rect = self.star_button.geometry()
            # Map click to frame coordinates
            click_pos = self.frame.mapFrom(self, event.pos())
            
            if not star_rect.contains(click_pos):
                self.original_item.clicked.emit(self.original_item.provider_name)
        super().mousePressEvent(event)
    
    def showEvent(self, event):
        """Start mouse position polling when shown."""
        super().showEvent(event)
        if self._check_timer is None:
            self._check_timer = QTimer(self)
            self._check_timer.timeout.connect(self._check_mouse_position)
        self._check_timer.start(50)  # Check every 50ms
    
    def hideEvent(self, event):
        """Stop polling when hidden."""
        if self._check_timer:
            self._check_timer.stop()
        super().hideEvent(event)
    
    def _check_mouse_position(self):
        """Check if mouse is still within valid area (popup or original item)."""
        if not self.isVisible():
            return
        
        cursor_pos = QCursor.pos()
        
        # Check if mouse is in this popup
        if self.geometry().contains(cursor_pos):
            return
        
        # Check if mouse is in original item (allow moving between them)
        if self.original_item and self.original_item.isVisible():
            item_global_rect = self.original_item.geometry()
            item_global_rect.moveTopLeft(self.original_item.mapToGlobal(self.original_item.rect().topLeft()))
            if item_global_rect.contains(cursor_pos):
                return
        
        # Mouse left both areas, hide
        self.hide()
    
    def _on_app_state_changed(self, state):
        """Hide when application loses focus."""
        if state != Qt.ApplicationActive:
            self.hide()
    
    def close(self):
        """Cleanup on close."""
        if self._check_timer:
            self._check_timer.stop()
            self._check_timer = None
        try:
            QApplication.instance().applicationStateChanged.disconnect(self._on_app_state_changed)
        except (TypeError, RuntimeError):
            pass  # Already disconnected or app closing
        super().close()


class ProviderItem(QFrame):
    """A single provider item in the provider list."""
    
    clicked = pyqtSignal(str)  # provider_name
    star_toggled = pyqtSignal(str, bool)  # provider_name, new_status
    
    def __init__(self, provider_name: str, is_starred: bool = False, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.provider_name = provider_name
        self._is_starred = is_starred
        self._is_selected = False
        self._is_hovered = False
        self._expansion_popup = None
        
        self.setObjectName("providerItem")
        self.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.setMouseTracking(True)
        self.init_ui()
        self._update_style()
    
    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(10, 8, 10, 8)
        layout.setSpacing(6)
        
        # Provider name
        self.name_label = QLabel(self.provider_name)
        self.name_label.setObjectName("providerName")
        font = QFont()
        font.setPointSize(9)
        self.name_label.setFont(font)
        layout.addWidget(self.name_label, 1)
        
        # Star button
        self.star_button = QPushButton()
        self.star_button.setObjectName("providerStarButton")
        self.star_button.setFixedSize(22, 22)
        self.star_button.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.star_button.clicked.connect(self._on_star_clicked)
        layout.addWidget(self.star_button)
        
        self._update_star_display()
        
        self.setMinimumHeight(38)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed)
    
    def _update_star_display(self):
        """Update star button appearance based on state."""
        if self._is_starred:
            self.star_button.setText("★")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton#providerStarButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #f5a623;
                }
                QPushButton#providerStarButton:hover {
                    color: #ffc107;
                }
            """)
            self.star_button.setVisible(True)
        elif self._is_hovered:
            self.star_button.setText("☆")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton#providerStarButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #999999;
                }
                QPushButton#providerStarButton:hover {
                    color: #666666;
                }
            """)
            self.star_button.setVisible(True)
        else:
            self.star_button.setVisible(False)
            
        if self._expansion_popup and self._expansion_popup.isVisible():
            self._expansion_popup._update_star_display()
    
    def _update_style(self):
        """Update appearance based on selection state."""
        # 1. Base style
        base_style = COMMON_BASE_STYLE + f"""
            QFrame#providerItem {{
                background-color: {Colors.NORMAL_BG};
                border: 1px solid transparent;
                border-radius: 4px;
            }}
        """

        # 2. State specific style
        if self._is_selected:
            status_style = f"""
                QFrame#providerItem {{
                    background-color: {Colors.SELECTED_BG};
                    border: 1px solid {Colors.SELECTED_BORDER};
                }}
                QLabel#providerName {{
                    color: {Colors.SELECTED_TEXT};
                }}
            """
        else:
            status_style = f"""
                QFrame#providerItem:hover {{
                    background-color: {Colors.HOVER_BG};
                    border-color: {Colors.HOVER_BORDER};
                }}
                QLabel#providerName {{
                    color: {Colors.TEXT_PRIMARY};
                }}
            """
        
        # 3. Combine
        self.setStyleSheet(base_style + status_style)
        
        if self._expansion_popup and self._expansion_popup.isVisible():
            self._expansion_popup.update_appearance()
    
    def _on_star_clicked(self):
        """Handle star button click."""
        self._is_starred = not self._is_starred
        self._update_star_display()
        self.star_toggled.emit(self.provider_name, self._is_starred)
    
    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            # Check if click was not on star button
            star_rect = self.star_button.geometry()
            if not star_rect.contains(event.pos()):
                self.clicked.emit(self.provider_name)
        super().mousePressEvent(event)
    
    def enterEvent(self, event):
        self._is_hovered = True
        self._update_star_display()
        self._check_show_expanded()
        super().enterEvent(event)
    
    def leaveEvent(self, event):
        self._is_hovered = False
        self._update_star_display()
        
        # Don't hide popup immediately - let it handle its own hide via timer
        # The popup's _check_mouse_position will handle hiding when mouse leaves both areas
        super().leaveEvent(event)
    
    def hideEvent(self, event):
        """Ensure popup is hidden when this item is hidden."""
        self._close_expansion_popup()
        super().hideEvent(event)
    
    def _close_expansion_popup(self):
        """Close and cleanup expansion popup."""
        if self._expansion_popup:
            self._expansion_popup.close()
            self._expansion_popup = None
        
    def _check_show_expanded(self):
        """Check if we need to show expanded popup."""
        # Check text width
        fm = self.name_label.fontMetrics()
        text_width = fm.boundingRect(self.provider_name).width()
        available_width = self.name_label.width()
        
        # If text is truncated or just for consistent "float out" effect
        if text_width > available_width:
            if not self._expansion_popup:
                self._expansion_popup = ExpandedProviderItem(self)
            
            # Position over the item
            global_pos = self.mapToGlobal(self.rect().topLeft())
            
            # Calculate required width
            req_width = text_width + 60 # padding + star button
            req_width = max(req_width, self.width())  # Ensure at least as wide as original
            
            self._expansion_popup.resize(req_width, self.height())
            self._expansion_popup.move(global_pos)
            self._expansion_popup.update_appearance()
            self._expansion_popup.show()

    def set_selected(self, selected: bool):
        """Set selection state."""
        if self._is_selected != selected:
            self._is_selected = selected
            self._update_style()
    
    def set_starred(self, starred: bool):
        """Set starred state without emitting signal."""
        if self._is_starred != starred:
            self._is_starred = starred
            self._update_star_display()
    
    def is_selected(self) -> bool:
        return self._is_selected
    
    def is_starred(self) -> bool:
        return self._is_starred


class ProviderList(QFrame):
    """Scrollable list of providers for the selected model."""
    
    provider_clicked = pyqtSignal(str)  # provider_name
    provider_star_toggled = pyqtSignal(str, bool)  # provider_name, new_status
    
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("providerList")
        self._provider_items: dict[str, ProviderItem] = {}
        self._current_selection: str | None = None
        self.init_ui()
    
    def init_ui(self):
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        main_layout.setSpacing(0)
        
        # Header
        header = QLabel("Providers:")
        header.setObjectName("providerListHeader")
        header.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel#providerListHeader {
                color: #666666;
                font-size: 10px;
                padding: 6px 8px;
                background-color: #f3f3f3;
                border-bottom: 1px solid #e0e0e0;
            }
        """)
        main_layout.addWidget(header)
        
        # Scroll area
        self.scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        self.scroll_area.setObjectName("providerScrollArea")
        self.scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.scroll_area.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.scroll_area.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAsNeeded)
        self.scroll_area.setStyleSheet("""
            QScrollArea#providerScrollArea {
                background-color: #f7f7f7;
                border: none;
            }
            QScrollBar:vertical {
                width: 8px;
                background: #f7f7f7;
            }
            QScrollBar::handle:vertical {
                background: #c0c0c0;
                border-radius: 4px;
                min-height: 20px;
            }
            QScrollBar::handle:vertical:hover {
                background: #a0a0a0;
            }
            QScrollBar::add-line:vertical, QScrollBar::sub-line:vertical {
                height: 0;
            }
        """)
        
        # Container for provider items
        self.container = QWidget()
        self.container.setObjectName("providerContainer")
        self.container.setStyleSheet("background-color: #f7f7f7;")
        self.items_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.container)
        self.items_layout.setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4)
        self.items_layout.setSpacing(4)
        self.items_layout.addStretch()
        
        # Placeholder
        self.placeholder = QLabel("Select a model")
        self.placeholder.setObjectName("providerPlaceholder")
        self.placeholder.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.placeholder.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel#providerPlaceholder {
                color: #999999;
                font-size: 10px;
                padding: 20px;
            }
        """)
        self.items_layout.insertWidget(0, self.placeholder)
        
        self.scroll_area.setWidget(self.container)
        main_layout.addWidget(self.scroll_area)
        
        self.setFixedWidth(140)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#providerList {
                background-color: #f7f7f7;
                border-left: 1px solid #e0e0e0;
            }
        """)
    
    def set_providers(self, providers: List[str], starred_provider: Optional[str] = None):
        """Set the list of providers to display."""
        # Clear existing items
        self._provider_items.clear()
        self._current_selection = None
        
        while self.items_layout.count() > 1:  # Keep stretch
            item = self.items_layout.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()
        
        if not providers:
            # Show placeholder
            self.placeholder = QLabel("No providers")
            self.placeholder.setObjectName("providerPlaceholder")
            self.placeholder.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
            self.placeholder.setStyleSheet("""
                QLabel#providerPlaceholder {
                    color: #999999;
                    font-size: 10px;
                    padding: 20px;
                }
            """)
            self.items_layout.insertWidget(0, self.placeholder)
            return
        
        # Add provider items
        for provider_name in providers:
            is_starred = provider_name == starred_provider
            item = ProviderItem(provider_name, is_starred=is_starred)
            item.clicked.connect(self._on_item_clicked)
            item.star_toggled.connect(self._on_star_toggled)
            self.items_layout.insertWidget(self.items_layout.count() - 1, item)
            self._provider_items[provider_name] = item
            
            # Auto-select starred provider
            if is_starred:
                self._current_selection = provider_name
                item.set_selected(True)
        
        # If no starred, select first
        if self._current_selection is None and providers:
            first_provider = providers[0]
            self._current_selection = first_provider
            self._provider_items[first_provider].set_selected(True)
    
    def _on_item_clicked(self, provider_name: str):
        """Handle provider item click."""
        self.select_provider(provider_name)
        self.provider_clicked.emit(provider_name)
    
    def _on_star_toggled(self, provider_name: str, new_status: bool):
        """Handle star toggle - ensure only one is starred."""
        if new_status:
            # Unstar all others
            for name, item in self._provider_items.items():
                if name != provider_name and item.is_starred():
                    item.set_starred(False)
        
        self.provider_star_toggled.emit(provider_name, new_status)
    
    def select_provider(self, provider_name: str):
        """Select a provider."""
        if self._current_selection == provider_name:
            return
        
        # Deselect previous
        if self._current_selection and self._current_selection in self._provider_items:
            self._provider_items[self._current_selection].set_selected(False)
        
        # Select new
        self._current_selection = provider_name
        if provider_name in self._provider_items:
            self._provider_items[provider_name].set_selected(True)
    
    def get_selected_provider(self) -> Optional[str]:
        return self._current_selection
    
    def get_starred_provider(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """Get currently starred provider name."""
        for name, item in self._provider_items.items():
            if item.is_starred():
                return name
        return None
```

```python
from PyQt5.QtWidgets import (

                    color: #999999;
                }
                QPushButton:hover {
                    color: #666666;
                }
            """)

    def _on_star_clicked(self):
        # Forward click to original item's logic
        self.original_item._on_star_clicked()
        self._update_star_display()

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            star_rect = self.star_button.geometry()
            # Map click to frame coordinates
            click_pos = self.frame.mapFrom(self, event.pos())
            
            if not star_rect.contains(click_pos):
                self.original_item.clicked.emit(self.original_item.provider_name)
        super().mousePressEvent(event)
    
    def showEvent(self, event):
        """Start mouse position polling when shown."""
        super().showEvent(event)
        if self._check_timer is None:
            self._check_timer = QTimer(self)
            self._check_timer.timeout.connect(self._check_mouse_position)
        self._check_timer.start(50)  # Check every 50ms
    
    def hideEvent(self, event):
        """Stop polling when hidden."""
        if self._check_timer:
            self._check_timer.stop()
        super().hideEvent(event)
    
    def _check_mouse_position(self):
        """Check if mouse is still within valid area (popup or original item)."""
        if not self.isVisible():
            return
        
        cursor_pos = QCursor.pos()
        
        # Check if mouse is in this popup
        if self.geometry().contains(cursor_pos):
            return
        
        # Check if mouse is in original item (allow moving between them)
        if self.original_item and self.original_item.isVisible():
            item_global_rect = self.original_item.geometry()
            item_global_rect.moveTopLeft(self.original_item.mapToGlobal(self.original_item.rect().topLeft()))
            if item_global_rect.contains(cursor_pos):
                return
        
        # Mouse left both areas, hide
        self.hide()
    
    def _on_app_state_changed(self, state):
        """Hide when application loses focus."""
        if state != Qt.ApplicationActive:
            self.hide()
    
    def close(self):
        """Cleanup on close."""
        if self._check_timer:
            self._check_timer.stop()
            self._check_timer = None
        try:
            QApplication.instance().applicationStateChanged.disconnect(self._on_app_state_changed)
        except (TypeError, RuntimeError):
            pass  # Already disconnected or app closing
        super().close()


class ProviderItem(QFrame):
    """A single provider item in the provider list."""
    
    clicked = pyqtSignal(str)  # provider_name
    star_toggled = pyqtSignal(str, bool)  # provider_name, new_status
    
    def __init__(self, provider_name: str, is_starred: bool = False, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.provider_name = provider_name
        self._is_starred = is_starred
        self._is_selected = False
        self._is_hovered = False
        self._expansion_popup = None
        
        self.setObjectName("providerItem")
        self.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.setMouseTracking(True)
        self.init_ui()
        self._update_style()
    
    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QHBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(10, 8, 10, 8)
        layout.setSpacing(6)
        
        # Provider name
        self.name_label = QLabel(self.provider_name)
        self.name_label.setObjectName("providerName")
        font = QFont()
        font.setPointSize(9)
        self.name_label.setFont(font)
        layout.addWidget(self.name_label, 1)
        
        # Star button
        self.star_button = QPushButton()
        self.star_button.setObjectName("providerStarButton")
        self.star_button.setFixedSize(22, 22)
        self.star_button.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.star_button.clicked.connect(self._on_star_clicked)
        layout.addWidget(self.star_button)
        
        self._update_star_display()
        
        self.setMinimumHeight(38)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed)
    
    def _update_star_display(self):
        """Update star button appearance based on state."""
        if self._is_starred:
            self.star_button.setText("★")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton#providerStarButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #f5a623;
                }
                QPushButton#providerStarButton:hover {
                    color: #ffc107;
                }
            """)
            self.star_button.setVisible(True)
        elif self._is_hovered:
            self.star_button.setText("☆")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton#providerStarButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #999999;
                }
                QPushButton#providerStarButton:hover {
                    color: #666666;
                }
            """)
            self.star_button.setVisible(True)
        else:
            self.star_button.setVisible(False)
            
        if self._expansion_popup and self._expansion_popup.isVisible():
            self._expansion_popup._update_star_display()
    
    def _update_style(self):
        """Update appearance based on selection state."""
        # 1. Base style
        base_style = COMMON_BASE_STYLE + f"""
            QFrame#providerItem {{
                background-color: {Colors.NORMAL_BG};
                border: 1px solid transparent;
                border-radius: 4px;
            }}
        """

        # 2. State specific style
        if self._is_selected:
            status_style = f"""
                QFrame#providerItem {{
                    background-color: {Colors.SELECTED_BG};
                    border: 1px solid {Colors.SELECTED_BORDER};
                }}
                QLabel#providerName {{
                    color: {Colors.SELECTED_TEXT};
                }}
            """
        else:
            status_style = f"""
                QFrame#providerItem:hover {{
                    background-color: {Colors.HOVER_BG};
                    border-color: {Colors.HOVER_BORDER};
                }}
                QLabel#providerName {{
                    color: {Colors.TEXT_PRIMARY};
                }}
            """
        
        # 3. Combine
        self.setStyleSheet(base_style + status_style)
        
        if self._expansion_popup and self._expansion_popup.isVisible():
            self._expansion_popup.update_appearance()
    
    def _on_star_clicked(self):
        """Handle star button click."""
        self._is_starred = not self._is_starred
        self._update_star_display()
        self.star_toggled.emit(self.provider_name, self._is_starred)
    
    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            # Check if click was not on star button
            star_rect = self.star_button.geometry()
            if not star_rect.contains(event.pos()):
                self.clicked.emit(self.provider_name)
        super().mousePressEvent(event)
    
    def enterEvent(self, event):
        self._is_hovered = True
        self._update_star_display()
        self._check_show_expanded()
        super().enterEvent(event)
    
    def leaveEvent(self, event):
        self._is_hovered = False
        self._update_star_display()
        
        # Don't hide popup immediately - let it handle its own hide via timer
        # The popup's _check_mouse_position will handle hiding when mouse leaves both areas
        super().leaveEvent(event)
    
    def hideEvent(self, event):
        """Ensure popup is hidden when this item is hidden."""
        self._close_expansion_popup()
        super().hideEvent(event)
    
    def _close_expansion_popup(self):
        """Close and cleanup expansion popup."""
        if self._expansion_popup:
            self._expansion_popup.close()
            self._expansion_popup = None
        
    def _check_show_expanded(self):
        """Check if we need to show expanded popup."""
        # Check text width
        fm = self.name_label.fontMetrics()
        text_width = fm.boundingRect(self.provider_name).width()
        available_width = self.name_label.width()
        
        # If text is truncated or just for consistent "float out" effect
        if text_width > available_width:
            if not self._expansion_popup:
                self._expansion_popup = ExpandedProviderItem(self)
            
            # Position over the item
            global_pos = self.mapToGlobal(self.rect().topLeft())
            
            # Calculate required width
            req_width = text_width + 60 # padding + star button
            req_width = max(req_width, self.width())  # Ensure at least as wide as original
            
            self._expansion_popup.resize(req_width, self.height())
            self._expansion_popup.move(global_pos)
            self._expansion_popup.update_appearance()
            self._expansion_popup.show()

    def set_selected(self, selected: bool):
        """Set selection state."""
        if self._is_selected != selected:
            self._is_selected = selected
            self._update_style()
    
    def set_starred(self, starred: bool):
        """Set starred state without emitting signal."""
        if self._is_starred != starred:
            self._is_starred = starred
            self._update_star_display()
    
    def is_selected(self) -> bool:
        return self._is_selected
    
    def is_starred(self) -> bool:
        return self._is_starred


class ProviderList(QFrame):
    """Scrollable list of providers for the selected model."""
    
    provider_clicked = pyqtSignal(str)  # provider_name
    provider_star_toggled = pyqtSignal(str, bool)  # provider_name, new_status
    
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setObjectName("providerList")
        self._provider_items: dict[str, ProviderItem] = {}
        self._current_selection: str | None = None
        self.init_ui()
    
    def init_ui(self):
        main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        main_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        main_layout.setSpacing(0)
        
        # Header
        header = QLabel("Providers:")
        header.setObjectName("providerListHeader")
        header.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel#providerListHeader {
                color: #666666;
                font-size: 10px;
                padding: 6px 8px;
                background-color: #f3f3f3;
                border-bottom: 1px solid #e0e0e0;
            }
        """)
        main_layout.addWidget(header)
        
        # Scroll area
        self.scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        self.scroll_area.setObjectName("providerScrollArea")
        self.scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(True)
        self.scroll_area.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.scroll_area.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAsNeeded)
        self.scroll_area.setStyleSheet("""
            QScrollArea#providerScrollArea {
                background-color: #f7f7f7;
                border: none;
            }
            QScrollBar:vertical {
                width: 8px;
                background: #f7f7f7;
            }
            QScrollBar::handle:vertical {
                background: #c0c0c0;
                border-radius: 4px;
                min-height: 20px;
            }
            QScrollBar::handle:vertical:hover {
                background: #a0a0a0;
            }
            QScrollBar::add-line:vertical, QScrollBar::sub-line:vertical {
                height: 0;
            }
        """)
        
        # Container for provider items
        self.container = QWidget()
        self.container.setObjectName("providerContainer")
        self.container.setStyleSheet("background-color: #f7f7f7;")
        self.items_layout = QVBoxLayout(self.container)
        self.items_layout.setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4)
        self.items_layout.setSpacing(4)
        self.items_layout.addStretch()
        
        # Placeholder
        self.placeholder = QLabel("Select a model")
        self.placeholder.setObjectName("providerPlaceholder")
        self.placeholder.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
        self.placeholder.setStyleSheet("""
            QLabel#providerPlaceholder {
                color: #999999;
                font-size: 10px;
                padding: 20px;
            }
        """)
        self.items_layout.insertWidget(0, self.placeholder)
        
        self.scroll_area.setWidget(self.container)
        main_layout.addWidget(self.scroll_area)
        
        self.setFixedWidth(140)
        self.setStyleSheet("""
            QFrame#providerList {
                background-color: #f7f7f7;
                border-left: 1px solid #e0e0e0;
            }
        """)
    
    def set_providers(self, providers: List[str], starred_provider: Optional[str] = None):
        """Set the list of providers to display."""
        # Close all expansion popups before clearing
        for item in self._provider_items.values():
            item._close_expansion_popup()
        
        # Clear existing items
        self._provider_items.clear()
        self._current_selection = None
        
        while self.items_layout.count() > 1:  # Keep stretch
            item = self.items_layout.takeAt(0)
            if item.widget():
                item.widget().deleteLater()
        
        if not providers:
            # Show placeholder
            self.placeholder = QLabel("No providers")
            self.placeholder.setObjectName("providerPlaceholder")
            self.placeholder.setAlignment(Qt.AlignCenter)
            self.placeholder.setStyleSheet("""
                QLabel#providerPlaceholder {
                    color: #999999;
                    font-size: 10px;
                    padding: 20px;
                }
            """)
            self.items_layout.insertWidget(0, self.placeholder)
            return
        
        # Add provider items
        for provider_name in providers:
            is_starred = provider_name == starred_provider
            item = ProviderItem(provider_name, is_starred=is_starred)
            item.clicked.connect(self._on_item_clicked)
            item.star_toggled.connect(self._on_star_toggled)
            self.items_layout.insertWidget(self.items_layout.count() - 1, item)
            self._provider_items[provider_name] = item
            
            # Auto-select starred provider
            if is_starred:
                self._current_selection = provider_name
                item.set_selected(True)
        
        # If no starred, select first
        if self._current_selection is None and providers:
            first_provider = providers[0]
            self._current_selection = first_provider
            self._provider_items[first_provider].set_selected(True)
    
    def _on_item_clicked(self, provider_name: str):
        """Handle provider item click."""
        self.select_provider(provider_name)
        self.provider_clicked.emit(provider_name)
    
    def _on_star_toggled(self, provider_name: str, new_status: bool):
        """Handle star toggle - ensure only one is starred."""
        if new_status:
            # Unstar all others
            for name, item in self._provider_items.items():
                if name != provider_name and item.is_starred():
                    item.set_starred(False)
        
        self.provider_star_toggled.emit(provider_name, new_status)
    
    def select_provider(self, provider_name: str):
        """Select a provider."""
        if self._current_selection == provider_name:
            return
        
        # Deselect previous
        if self._current_selection and self._current_selection in self._provider_items:
            self._provider_items[self._current_selection].set_selected(False)
        
        # Select new
        self._current_selection = provider_name
        if provider_name in self._provider_items:
            self._provider_items[provider_name].set_selected(True)
    
    def get_selected_provider(self) -> Optional[str]:
        return self._current_selection
    
    def get_starred_provider(self) -> Optional[str]:
        """Get currently starred provider name."""
        for name, item in self._provider_items.items():
            if item.is_starred():
                return name
        return None

```

```python
"""
Provider List Widget - Right column showing available providers for selected model.
"""

from PyQt5.QtWidgets import (
    QFrame, QVBoxLayout, QLabel, QScrollArea, QWidget, QHBoxLayout, 
    QPushButton, QSizePolicy, QApplication
)
from PyQt5.QtCore import Qt, pyqtSignal, QEvent, QTimer
from PyQt5.QtGui import QFont, QCursor
from typing import List, Optional

from .styles import ModelSelectorColors as Colors, COMMON_BASE_STYLE


class ExpandedProviderItem(QWidget):
    """Floating window for displaying full provider name when truncated."""
    
    def __init__(self, original_item, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.original_item = original_item
        self._check_timer = None
        
        self.setWindowFlags(Qt.ToolTip | Qt.FramelessWindowHint)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_TranslucentBackground)
        self.setAttribute(Qt.WA_ShowWithoutActivating)
        
        self.init_ui()
        self.update_appearance()
        
        # Monitor application state changes (window switching)
        QApplication.instance().applicationStateChanged.connect(self._on_app_state_changed)
        
    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        
        self.frame = QFrame()
        self.frame.setObjectName("expandedProviderFrame")
        layout.addWidget(self.frame)
        
        frame_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.frame)
        frame_layout.setContentsMargins(10, 8, 10, 8)
        frame_layout.setSpacing(6)
        
        # Provider name
        self.name_label = QLabel(self.original_item.provider_name)
        font = QFont()
        font.setPointSize(9)
        self.name_label.setFont(font)
        frame_layout.addWidget(self.name_label)
        
        # Star button
        self.star_button = QPushButton()
        self.star_button.setFixedSize(22, 22)
        self.star_button.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
        self.star_button.clicked.connect(self._on_star_clicked)
        frame_layout.addWidget(self.star_button)
        
        self._update_star_display()
        
    def update_appearance(self):
        # Copy style from original item but ensure we have a background and shadow
        is_selected = self.original_item.is_selected()
        
        bg_color = Colors.SELECTED_BG if is_selected else Colors.HOVER_BG
        border_color = Colors.SELECTED_BORDER if is_selected else Colors.HOVER_BORDER
        text_color = Colors.SELECTED_TEXT if is_selected else Colors.TEXT_PRIMARY
        
        self.frame.setStyleSheet(f"""
            QFrame#expandedProviderFrame {{
                background-color: {bg_color};
                border: 1px solid {border_color};
                border-radius: 4px;
            }}
            QLabel {{
                color: {text_color};
            }}
        """)
        
        self._update_star_display()
        
    def _update_star_display(self):
        is_starred = self.original_item.is_starred()
        
        if is_starred:
            self.star_button.setText("★")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #f5a623;
                }
                QPushButton:hover {
                    color: #ffc107;
                }
            """)
        else:
            self.star_button.setText("☆")
            self.star_button.setStyleSheet("""
                QPushButton {
                    background: transparent;
                    border: none;
                    font-size: 14px;
                    color: #999999;
                }
                QPushButton:hover {
                    color: #666666;
                }
            """)

    def _on_star_clicked(self):
        # Forward click to original item's logic
        self.original_item._on_star_clicked()
        self._update_star_display()

    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            star_rect = self.star_button.geometry()
            # Map click to frame coordinates
            click_pos = self.frame.mapFrom(self, event.pos())
            
            if not star_rect.contains(click_pos):
                self.original_item.clicked.emit(self.original_item.provider_name)
        super().mousePressEvent(event)
    
    def showEvent(self, event):
        """Start mouse position polling when shown."""
        super().showEvent(event)
        if self._check_timer is None:
            self._check_timer = QTimer(self)
            self._check_timer.timeout.connect(self._check_mouse_position)
        self._check_timer.start(50)  # Check every 50ms
    
    def hideEvent(self, event):
        """Stop polling when hidden."""
        if self._check_timer:
            self._check_timer.stop()
        super().hideEvent(event)
    
    def _check_mouse_position(self):
        """Check if mouse is still within valid area (popup or original item)."""
        if not self.isVisible():
            return
        
        cursor_pos = QCursor.pos()
        
        # Check if mouse is in this popup
        if self.geometry().contains(cursor_pos):
            return
        
        # Check if mouse is in original item (allow moving between them)
        if self.original_item and self.original_item.isVisible():
            item_global_rect = self.original_item.geometry()
            item_global_rect.moveTopLeft(self.original_item.mapToGlobal(self.original_item.rect().topLeft()))
            if item_global_rect.contains(cursor_pos):
                return
        
        # Mouse left both areas, hide
        self.hide()
    
    def _on_app_state_changed(self, state):
        """Hide when application loses focus."""
        if state != Qt.ApplicationActive:
            self.hide()
    
    def close(self):
        """Cleanup on close."""
        if self._check_timer:
            self._check_timer.stop()
            self._check_timer = None
        try:
            QApplication.instance().applicationStateChanged.disconnect(self._on_app_state_changed)
        except (TypeError, RuntimeError):
            pass  # Already disconnected or app closing
        super().close()

                }}

                    color: {Colors.TEXT_PRIMARY};
                }}
            """
        
        # 3. Combine
        self.setStyleSheet(base_style + status_style)
        
        if self._expansion_popup and self._expansion_popup.isVisible():
            self._expansion_popup.update_appearance()
    
    def _on_star_clicked(self):
        """Handle star button click."""
        self._is_starred = not self._is_starred
        self._update_star_display()
        self.star_toggled.emit(self.provider_name, self._is_starred)
    
    def mousePressEvent(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            # Check if click was not on star button
            star_rect = self.star_button.geometry()
```
